"Abstract" - 摘要

"We introduce a novel method for verifying Lean formalizations that goes beyond LLM-judged faithfulness." - 我们提出了一种用于验证Lean形式化的新颖方法，该方法超越了基于LLM评审的忠实性判断。

"Unlike most verification methods, which rely on LLMs or trained models, our method combines LLM-based semantic review with mechanical numerical and vacuity checks, providing checkable feedback and detecting errors missed by LLM judges." - 与大多数依赖LLM或训练模型的验证方法不同，我们的方法将基于LLM的语义审查与机械的数值和空真性检查相结合，提供可检验的反馈，并检测LLM评审遗漏的错误。

"We apply this method, along with our Extract-Verify-Proof autoformalization pipeline, to construct the Formalized Library of Mathematical Functions (FLMF), a partial formalization of the NIST Digital Library of Mathematical Functions (DLMF)." - 我们将该方法与我们的"提取-验证-证明"（Extract-Verify-Proof）自动形式化流水线相结合，构建了数学函数形式化库（Formalized Library of Mathematical Functions，FLMF），这是NIST数学函数数字图书馆（DLMF）的部分形式化。

"On three labeled datasets, our method achieves the lowest accepted-error rate among the evaluated methods, measured as the fraction of incorrect statements accepted by the verification method." - 在三个带标注的数据集上，我们的方法在所有被评估方法中实现了最低的接受错误率，该指标以被验证方法接受的不正确命题所占比例来衡量。

"Expert review of the results also identifies translation and source defects in existing datasets." - 对结果的专家评审还发现了现有数据集中的翻译缺陷和源文献缺陷。

"Code available at https://github.com/annamei1/FLMF-Ch7ErrFunc-Release.git" - 代码可在 https://github.com/annamei1/FLMF-Ch7ErrFunc-Release.git 获取

Let me translate the content:

"Lean statements, which are mechanical and provide checkable feedback on points of failure. As indicated by their names, a numerical check verifies numerical validity of a Lean statement, and a vacuity check looks for vacuous formalization, for example a tautological definition. So far, all verification in autoformalization relies on LLM judgements, and our method complements the ambiguity and arbitrariness of an LLM judge's verdicts. Its feedback is tractable and informative, and passing these checks adds another layer of certainty about the correctness of a Lean statement."

Translation:
"Lean 语句是机械性的，并针对失败点提供可检查的反馈。正如其名称所示，数值检查验证 Lean 语句的数值有效性，而空洞性检查则寻找空洞的形式化，例如同义反复的定义。迄今为止，自动形式化中的所有验证都依赖于 LLM 判断，而我们的方法弥补了 LLM 评判者裁决的模糊性和任意性。其反馈是可处理且信息丰富的，通过这些检查为 Lean 语句的正确性增加了另一层确定性。"

"•
Autoformalization pipeline
(Section
4
). Our pipeline divides the formalization process into three stages: extract, verify, and prove. We emphasize human gating at each stage, requiring every item either to pass a gate or to be documented with a reason for failure. We then use an auditor agent to revise those documents against written guidelines. We also keep track of all statement corrections to allow future studies of them."

Translation:
"•
自动形式化流水线
（第
4
节）。我们的流水线将形式化过程分为三个阶段：提取、验证和证明。我们强调在每个阶段进行人工把关，要求每个条目要么通过关卡，要么记录其失败原因。然后，我们使用一个审计代理根据书面指南修订这些文档。我们还跟踪所有语句修正，以便未来对其进行研究。"

"•
Corrections to existing datasets
(Section
5
). Expert review of our verification flags identifies multiple defects in existing datasets. Among the statements that are labeled correct, we found 4 defects among ProofNet items labeled faithful by Ground False, 61 defects among correct-labeled Beyond Compilation items, and 224 defects in RLM25 (Table
3
). We will release corrected statements for confirmed defects."

Let me put it all together, keeping the Markdown format (bullet points, section headers, etc.):Lean 语句是机械性的，并针对失败点提供可检查的反馈。正如其名称所示，数值检查验证 Lean 语句的数值有效性，而空洞性检查则寻找空洞的形式化，例如同义反复的定义。迄今为止，自动形式化中的所有验证都依赖于 LLM 判断，而我们的方法弥补了 LLM 评判者裁决的模糊性和任意性。其反馈是可处理且信息丰富的，通过这些检查为 Lean 语句的正确性增加了另一层确定性。
•
自动形式化流水线
（第
4
节）。我们的流水线将形式化过程分为三个阶段：提取、验证和证明。我们强调在每个阶段进行人工把关，要求每个条目要么通过关卡，要么记录其失败原因。然后，我们使用一个审计代理根据书面指南修订这些文档。我们还跟踪所有语句修正，以便未来对其进行研究。
•

5

3
）。我们将发布已确认缺陷的修正语句。

Let me translate the content:

"s use reference-based metrics to evaluate the faithfulness of formalization: BEq and its variants check bidirectional provable equivalence (Liu et al., 2025c; Poiroux et al., 2025a), GTED compares operator trees (Liu et al., 2025b), and ASSESS combines semantic and structural similarity (Liu et al., 2026). These metrics need a reference formal statement, which is a limitation when it comes to the formalization of natural language statements. On the other hand, reference-free methods score the informal–formal pair directly. FormalAlign trains a model to predict alignment from sequence certainty and representation similarity (Lu et al., 2025a). LeanScorer breaks a statement into components and has an LLM judge each one (Xuejun et al., 2025). Beyond Compilation evaluates LLM judges for faithful natural-language-to-Lean translation (Zhang et al., 2026). FormalMATH uses back-translation followed by LLM judgments to filter formalizations that do not preserve the original problem (Yu et al., 2025). As a result, Poiroux et al. show that automatic metrics disagree with humans on research-level statements and release RLM25 (Poiroux et al., 2025a). Ground False audits formal benchmarks for kernel-checked but wrong targets, classifies each ProofNet statement's relation to its informal source, and releases both that taxonomy and a corrected ProofNet-Verified (An et al., 2026). In this paper, we compare our verification method against them using formal–informal"

数值检查也存在局限性。由于我们处理的是 DLMF 中的特殊函数，我们只能对定义域中的点进行采样，并在这些点上计算函数值。一个常见的失败情况是无法识别函数等式中错误的定义域。例如，Lean 陈述的定义域可能比实际陈述的定义域更小，或者可能未能从定义域中排除孤立的点。如果这些点没有被数值检查器选中，它就无法标记出错误。因此，数值检查只能作为正确性的部分检查手段，而不能作为完全的忠实性检查。
3.3
空洞性检查
空洞性检查会扫描定理陈述和证明主体，以检测空洞的陈述。简单的检查包括测试诸如
rfl
或
simp
之类的平凡证明方法是否能够关闭目标。此外，我们还有一个检查器用于扫描与目标无关的证明，其工作原理是将目标结论替换为一个不透明的结论
VacQ
，然后检查证明是否仍然能够编译通过。如果能够编译通过，那么该证明就是与目标无关的，最可能的原因是存在一组相互矛盾的假设，从中可以推导出
False
3
3
3
任何结论都可以从
False
推导得出。
。如果检查器标记出一个空洞的证明，
verified
状态也会被回退为
unverified
。
我们想强调的是，这些检查中没有一个是完美的；它们各自具有不同的有效范围和局限性

当一个定理首次被陈述时，其验证状态默认为 unverified（未验证）。成功的验证检查（即第 3 节中讨论的语义、数值和空洞性检查的组合）允许盖章者将此标签设置为 verified（已验证）。如果定理未通过验证检查，智能体会分析失败的原因。对于容易修复的问题，例如缺失前提、符号错误或约定不兼容，智能体会修复定理并重新运行验证检查；否则将转交人工审查。此阶段设有门控，要求每个方程要么 拥有 verified（已验证）标记，要么 在 HUMAN_REVIEW.md 中有记录条目。如果某个陈述确实得到更正，它将被记录在勘误（ERRATA）文件中，以追踪智能体所犯的错误，并允许进一步的

Having the auditor significantly reduces the work of a human expert. The human expert can focus on the hard and subtle cases in which we do not readily trust the model's judgement, while the rest are taken care of autonomously. Having an auditor does not reduce the reliability of the output, since the edited statement will have to go through the verification check again before it can be stamped

verified

→ 我们包含了一些有意设计为不正确的变异，变异机制提取自历史错误

ProofNet uses the original release (Azerbayev et al., 2023), with Ground False's faithfulness verdicts as labels (An et al., 2026). It contains 371 undergraduate math problems from analysis, algebra, and topology.
→ ProofNet 使用原始发布版本（Azerbayev 等，2023），并以 Ground False 的忠实性判定作为标签（An 等，2026）。它包含 371 道来自分析、代数和拓扑的本科数学问题。

Beyond Compilation is a set of 400 graduate-level math problems with semantic evaluations (Zhang et al., 2026).
→ Beyond Compilation 是一组包含语义评估的 400 道研究生级别数学问题（Zhang 等，2026）。

RLM25 extracts 617 informal--formal statement pairs from six research-level formalization projects across number theory, harmonic analysis, combinatorics, etc (Poiroux et al., 2025a).
→ RLM25 从数论、调和分析、组合数学等领域的六个研究级形式化项目中提取了 617 对非形式化-形式化陈述对（Poiroux 等，2025a）。

Footnote 5: RLM25 doesn't contain any evaluation component, so we assume all statements are labeled "correct".
→ RLM25 不包含任何评估组件，因此我们假设所有陈述都被标记为"正确"。

Appendix D gives a more detailed breakdown of dataset construction process.
→ 附录 D 给出了数据集构建过程的更详细分解。

Methods and models. Ours is the union of the three checks as discussed in Section 3, where direct inspection uses GPT-5.5, and back-translation uses Gemini 3.1 Pro as the translator and GPT-5.5 as the judge.
→ 方法与模型。我们的方法是第 3 节中讨论的三种检查的并集，其中直接检查使用 GPT-5.5，回译使用 Gemini 3.1 Pro 作为翻译器，GPT-5.5 作为评判器。

The baseline methods are GPT-5.5 and Opus 4.6 as direct judges, each called once per pair and once in joint batches of 16 ("joint"); LeanScorer (Xuejun et al., 2025), which uses LLMs
→ 基线方法是作为直接评判器的 GPT-5.5 和 Opus 4.6，每个方法对每对调用一次，并在 16 个一批的联合批次中调用一次（"联合"）；LeanScorer（Xuejun 等，2025），它使用 LLM

"(GPT-5.5) to assess how faithfully each component of a statement is formalized; and FormalAlign (Lu et al., 2025a), a trained model that evaluates alignment between informal and formal statements, at a certainty threshold of 0.3 (Appendix E gives more detail). Every method sees the same informal statement and formal context."

Translation: "(GPT-5.5) 来评估陈述的每个组成部分被形式化的忠实程度；以及 FormalAlign (Lu et al., 2025a)，这是一个经过训练的模型，用于评估非形式化陈述与形式化陈述之间的对齐程度，其确定性阈值设为 0.3（附录 E 提供了更多细节）。每种方法都会看到相同的非形式化陈述和形式化上下文。"

"Method coverage. The semantic checks, direct LLM judges, LeanScorer, and FormalAlign apply to every informal–formal pair. However, the goal-independent part of the vacuity check requires a completed proof and has no coverage on the original ProofNet or Beyond Compilation. In these cases, the vacuity check only looks for trivial proof methods. Similarly, the numerical check requires a statement to be numerically evaluable, which is not the case for, for example, infinite sums statements."

Translation: "方法覆盖范围。语义检查、直接 LLM 评判器、LeanScorer 和 FormalAlign 适用于每一对非形式化-形式化配对。然而，空洞性检查中与目标无关的部分需要完整的证明，在原始 ProofNet 或 Beyond Compilation 上没有覆盖。在这些情况下，空洞性检查仅查找平凡的证明方法。类似地，数值检查要求陈述可进行数值评估，例如无穷级数陈述就不满足这一条件。"

"Metric. We evaluate acceptance by a verification method against each dataset's labels, which are categorized into "correct" and "incorrect". Our primary metric is the accepted-error rate: among the items a verification method accepts (does not flag), the fraction that are labeled incorrect. This metric aligns with our goal of ensuring that a Lean statement passing the gate is correct. We also report the percentage of total statements flagged."

Translation: "评估指标。我们根据每个数据集的标签来评估验证方法的接受情况，这些标签被分为"正确"和"错误"两类。我们的主要指标是接受错误率：在验证方法接受（未标记）的项目中，被标记为错误的项目所占的比例。该指标与我们的目标一致，即确保通过关卡的 Lean 陈述是正确的。我们还报告了被标记的陈述占总陈述的百分比。"

The user wants me to translate English content to Chinese, keeping Markdown format, URLs, and image links. The content appears to be from an academic paper about autoformalization and Lean theorem proving.

Let me translate the content:

"re complementary to the verdicts of an LLM or model-based judge, the most common approach used across all other formalization work. According to our experiments, these checks are able to spot mistakes ignored by all LLM judges, including subtle translation defects or informal source errors. The checks are also informative in pointing out the exact numerical misalignment or proof inconsistency. A theorem passing these checks can be trusted to be at least numerically consistent and not trivially vacuous. Combined with our autoformalization pipeline, we can then obtain a promising Lean formalization of the informal source.
There are certain limitations to our current methods. Numerical and vacuity checks are only applicable to numerically evaluable theorems or complete proofs. There could be defects in these checks as well, for example, a wrong binding in the numerical check or a subtly vacuous proof that does not fall within the detection range of our vacuity detector. Passing these checks doesn't guarantee correctness either, for example a narrower domain in the Lean statement compared to the informal source is a common failure mode.
Future work will extend the numerical and vacuity checks to handle additional corner cases encountered during formalization, while guaranteeing faithfulness of the checks. We aim to reduce vacuity false alarms by distinguishing contradictory assumptions and missing mathematical content from legitimate definitional identities and routine proofs. We"

Translation:

"（这些检查）是对 LLM 或基于模型的评判器（这是所有其他形式化工作中最常用的方法）判定结果的补充。根据我们的实验，这些检查能够发现所有 LLM 评判器忽略的错误，包括细微的翻译缺陷或非正式源文本中的错误。这些检查还能有效地指出具体的数值不一致或证明不一致之处。通过这些检查的定理至少可以被认为是数值一致的，且不是平凡空洞的。结合我们的自动形式化流程，我们可以获得对非正式源文本的一个有前景的 Lean 形式化。

我们目前的方法存在一定的局限性。数值检查和空洞性检查仅适用于可数值求值的定理或完整的证明。这些检查本身也可能存在缺陷，例如数值检查中的错误绑定，或者不在我们空洞性检测器检测范围内的微妙空洞证明。通过这些检查也不能保证正确性，例如 Lean 陈述中的定义域比非正式源文本更窄就是一种常见的失败模式。

未来的工作将扩展数值检查和空洞性检查，以处理形式化过程中遇到的更多边界情况，同时保证检查的忠实性。我们旨在通过区分矛盾的假设和缺失的数学内容与合法的定义恒等式和常规证明，来减少空洞性误报。我们"

"（这些检查）与 LLM 或基于模型的评判器的判定结果形成互补，后者是所有其他形式化工作中最常用的方法。根据我们的实验，这些检查能够发现所有 LLM 评判器都忽略的错误，包括细微的翻译缺陷或非正式源文本中的错误。这些检查还能提供有用的信息，指出具体的数值偏差或证明不一致之处。通过这些检查的定理至少可以确信在数值上是一致的，且并非平凡地空洞。结合我们的自动形式化流程，我们便可以获得对非正式源文本的一个有前景的 Lean 形式化。
